# Phase 13 figure inventory

Prepared 2026-10-11 on branch `phase-13-prep` (based on `main` @ `3982925`), read-only. The DTap figures were updated
the same day to the phase-10b full census (branch `phase-13-dtap`, based on `main` @ `d0d4b7f`), and D-26 in
DECISIONS.md was updated to the census; AGENTS.md citations were then remapped to `main` @ `33c5850` (4 lines inserted
at e81da95). Machine-readable copy: [`figure-inventory.json`](figure-inventory.json) (schema
`agentprov-phase13/figure-inventory/1`; one record per line under `figures`).

This is the checklist for phase 13 (full-corpus regression, PQ row 13): every number the manuscript and the
specification rely on, where it is stated now, what produces it, and what is expected to change it before phase 13
runs. Phase 13 should walk this list instead of searching for figures. No value here was recomputed. Every value is
copied as stated, and where a value or its source could not be found, the record says so.

## 1. Summary

- **1,150 figure records** from six sweeps (spec D and I; the other spec sections, including O and PQ; the per-source
  reports; the phase-10 analysis; examination 2 and the systematic search; README and AGENTS.md), plus a map of the
  gates and imports. 116 records carry a phase-13 marker from the source text ("recompute: phase 13", "phase 13",
  "measured in the phase-13 full-corpus run" and similar).
- **36 key figures** (section 5) carry the manuscript and the claim gates: baseline gates A1/A2/B/G0, gate R, gate E
  and the semantic-diff rows, the eight phase-10 imports, ROPE Q.8, SEM taint counts, the derive scan, evidence-class
  composition, field-group coverage, linkage, recompute, lineage, the coverage phrase, the search yields, and DTap
  (full census: 67,808 trajectory files, 65,902 judge results; limitation: 57.3% of successful indirect attacks
  unobservable).
- **183 records have no producing command** in the repository (97 "none yet", 86 "unknown"). The structural gaps are
  in section 8.1. Most fall to phase 11 (`stats`, `recompute`, `verify`, `validation/`) and phase 14 (values read from
  the gate-E report, the coverage phrase). Field-group coverage, the paper's headline loss metric, has **no value
  anywhere** yet.
- **35 distinct inconsistencies** (section 9), none resolved here. The ones that touch cited numbers: B1 (1,166 vs
  1,235 / 1,840); stale migration reports (dev mode, old lock) against AGENTS.md; the args key-order and integral-float
  hazard counts, which differ across EF, J and K; checksum lists (25 vs 23); the cross-check coverage claim (89,390
  vs 85,751); DTap's run count in card §3 (64,110, superseded) vs the census (67,808), with §3's 'unlabelled' runs and §1's seed.sql claim also contradicted by the census (to route).
- **Gates without code:** gate D full has no command or test, and it blocks the CLI route of gate E. R3 is not
  implemented. `recompute` and `verify` are not registered. Gate E full exists as a test but has never run.
- **Manuscript sources:** `~/Desktop/PhD/Labs/3. AgentPROV paper/` does not exist (checked
  2026-10-11), so no manuscript figure could be swept. Manuscript destinations below come from spec O (value keys,
  claims, figures F1–F9, tables). The thesis repository (`0. thesis`) has no AgentPROV chapter source.

## 2. Scope, method and limits

**Swept** (read only):
- `docs/architecture/` (all sections; PQ and O first, including the O.3.5 pipeline and the value-key list);
- `docs/reports/**` (the per-source reports, `sem/`, `prov/`, `migration/`, `live/`, `analysis/` with
  examination 2 and its data, `systematic-search/`); for DTap also the census section of
  `systematic-search/candidates/dtap-bench.md` (§6) and the DTap rows of `analysis/datasheet-items.md`;
- `README.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/compat/`, `docs/ns/`, `docs/review/`;
- the phase-10 run logs and summaries in `~/.agentprov/migration-work/phase10-runs/` (logs only, no corpora).

Nothing under `~/.agentprov/corpora/` was opened. No import, test or analysis tool was run.

**Method.**
- Six Sonnet subagents swept disjoint scopes under one brief. Values were copied verbatim and line numbers found by
  search. Each record names its producer (verified with `ls`) and its regeneration-batch, DTap and manuscript impact.
- A seventh subagent mapped the gates, imports and environment variables from the CLI, test and migration code.
- Every cited location was then re-checked mechanically (`location_check` in the JSON; section 7 has the totals).
- The key figures, gaps, inconsistencies and order of operations were assembled by hand from the records. Each key
  figure lists the records it draws on.

**What the impact fields mean.**
- `regen_impact`: the items of [`../analysis/regen-batch.md`](../analysis/regen-batch.md) as of `3982925`, items 1–12.
  Under D-27 (c6), the nine document-only class-D rules join item 3. B1 was re-measured at 1,235 executions / 1,840
  markers.
- `dtap_impact`: D-26. DTap adds one source and corpus. It changes only cross-source figures, such as the coverage
  phrase, the `all/` min/max values and claims about every corpus. Per D-26, no existing corpus digest changes.
- `phase12_impact`: phase 12 had no commits beyond `main` on 2026-10-11. A phase-12 fix could matter only where it
  changes which inputs `ingest/`, `raw/` or `worker.py` accept, and such a fix must first be entered in the
  regeneration batch.
- `manuscript_destination`: spec O value keys, claims, figures and tables. "unknown" where O names no destination.

**Limits.**
- The sweeps cover numbers a table or claim could cite. Test-suite pass counts, line counts, versions, digests and
  resource limits were skipped by design.
- Where one figure is stated in several scopes, each sweep made its own record. The key figures in section 5 join
  them, but the long tail in section 6 is not deduplicated across sweeps.
- 207 of 3,286 locations did not confirm mechanically (section 7). They are kept, marked `not-confirmed`, and must be
  checked before use.
- The values reflect `main` at `3982925`. Phases 10b, 11 and 12 are moving, so re-check any figure whose stating file
  has changed since. The DTap records were re-checked against `d0d4b7f`; the non-DTap rows of
  `analysis/datasheet-items.md` (added after the sweep) are not inventoried, except the 14,182 overlap (row 3).

## 3. Order of operations for phase 13

This order assumes phase 13 runs once on the final commit. It is a proposal for the phase-13 session; nothing in it has been run.

**Preconditions**

- Phase 11 merged: `derive`, `recompute`, `verify` (levels), `package`, `stats` (counts, statements, unknowns, field_groups, --artefacts), corpus `validation/`.
- Phase 10b merged: `dtap` adapter, profile, catalogue and fixtures (D-26 to D-29).
- Phase 12 and 12b merged: any fix in `ingest/`, `raw/` or `worker.py` that changes accepted bytes or seals is first recorded in regen-batch.md.
- Regeneration batch approved by the user (items 1-12 plus c6 joined to item 3) and applied with its expected digest changes stated; golden PROV and gate-D pins regenerated with approval (`AGENTPROV_REGEN_PROV_DIGESTS=1`, `AGENTPROV_REGEN_GATE_D_DIGESTS=1`).
- Missing gate code decided and merged: gate D full (`migrate gate-d` or a full_corpus test), gate R3 (`migration.reconcile`).
- Final commit fixed: clean tree, `uv sync --locked`, CPython 3.14.8, `uv lock --check`. Record the commit in every report. Never change `src/` between a pause and a resume of an import.

**1. Fast checks (minutes)**

- `uv run pytest` (default suite, including the golden A1/A2/B, D, E and R tests); `uv run ruff check .`; `uv run mypy`; `uv run python tools/check_unicode.py`.

**2. Baseline migration gates (release mode; needs AGENTPROV_BASELINE_CORPUS, AGENTPROV_BASELINE_PYTHON or _WHEEL)**

- G0: `uv run agentprov migrate verify-baseline --scope full --release --report docs/reports/migration/G0.json`.
- A1/A2: `uv run agentprov migrate gate-a --scope full --workers 10 --release --report docs/reports/migration/A-full.json` (phase 4: 736 s).
- B: `uv run agentprov migrate gate-b --scope full --workers 10 --release --report docs/reports/migration/B-full.json` (phase 4: 759 s).
- D full: the command phase 11/13 provides (none today).
- E full: `uv run agentprov migrate gate-e --scope full --workers 10 --release --report docs/reports/migration/E-full.json --semdiff-dir migration/semdiff`, cross-checked by `uv run pytest tests/full_corpus/test_baseline_gates.py::test_semdiff_all -m full_corpus`. Then `tools/gen_semdiff_tables.py` for docs/compat/semantic-diff.md.

**3. Census (needs AGENTPROV_UPSTREAM_CACHE)**

- `uv run pytest tests/full_corpus/test_profile_census.py -m full_corpus` (head took 909.7 s, 2024-06 66.1 s). Census counts do not change with the batch; a difference here is a snapshot or profile problem.

**4. Full-snapshot imports, release mode, sequential, `--jobs 10` (~6.5 h for the eight phase-10 steps, plus DTap)**

- Quick ones first to catch a broken build early: agentharm (134 s), mandate (58 s), survey agentdojo-runs (525 s).
- Then agentdojo 2024-06 (2,564 s), adprov-v1 release (2,713 s), rope (3,015 s), agentdojo head (5,186 s), agentdyn a (9,145 s), dtap (multi-hour; not measured).
- Use new corpus paths (e.g. `*-p13`). Never overwrite the p10 corpora; other sessions compare against them.

**5. Gates on the new corpora**

- Gate R full (R1, R2, R4, R5) on the final 2024-06: `uv run python tools/agentdojo/import_scan.py --out docs/reports/agentdojo --workers 10` or `uv run pytest tests/full_corpus/test_upstream_pinned_import.py::test_gate_r_full -m full_corpus`. R3 once implemented.
- Q.11a: `uv run pytest tests/full_corpus/test_inspect_survey.py -m full_corpus` (needs AGENTPROV_SURVEY_CLONE).
- Per corpus: `agentprov validate`, `agentprov recompute --corpus <c> --check` (0 diff), `agentprov verify --corpus <c> --level chain`; two `package` runs byte-identical (Q.20). A second full import of at least one large corpus with a different `--jobs` (determinism across worker counts).

**6. Statistics and reports**

- `agentprov stats --corpus <c> --tables counts,statements,unknowns,field_groups --format json` per corpus, and `--artefacts`; write to docs/reports/<source>/ and docs/reports/artefacts/.
- Per-source reports: `tools/import_report.py --summary <step>.summary.json`, `tools/sem/sem_import_report.py`, `tools/agentdojo/{rope_report,sem_unknown_tools,source_checks}.py`, `tools/agentdojo/sem_equivalence_scan.py`, `tools/prov/derive_scan.py` (baseline).
- Re-run the analysis tools only for figures the manuscript cites (tools/analysis/phase10_*.py, tools/analysis2/exam2_*.py); they read corpora read-only.

**7. Walk this inventory**

- For every record: write the recomputed value next to the stated one; a difference is resolved by finding its cause (batch item, DTap, phase-12 fix, or a documentation error), then updating the stating document under the ownership rules. Close every inconsistency in section 9 explicitly.
- Manual CI dispatch before the paper reports live or migration results (K.19.1 point 3).

### 4.2 Full-snapshot imports

Commands as run in phase 10 by `~/.agentprov/migration-work/phase10-runs/run_all.sh` (with `UP=~/Desktop/PhD/Labs/AgentPROV-upstream`, `C=$HOME/.agentprov/corpora`, `OUT=$HOME/.agentprov/migration-work/phase10-runs`). Durations are the wall seconds in that directory's `progress.log` (M2 Max, `--jobs 10`).

| Step | Source · part | `--snapshot` | Command | Phase-10 wall time | Phase-10 counts | Report directory |
